Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-060-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMember ;
        sh:class nltl:structuralMember ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-060-MemberShape>
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A member in way of a cut-out must not be unstable." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?member nltl:memberInWayOfCutout true ;
                        nltl:hasCutout ?cutout .
                $this nltl:memberInstability true .
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Structural stiffening must be recorded where stiffening is necessary." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?member nltl:memberInWayOfCutout true ;
                        nltl:stiffeningNecessary true .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:structuralStiffening ?stiffening .
                    FILTER (datatype(?stiffening) = xsd:string)
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-060-MemberShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:memberInWayOfCutout ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:memberInWayOfCutout ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasCutout ;
                sh:class nltl:cutout ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "An applicable member must identify its cut-out."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:stiffeningNecessary ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "An applicable member must state whether stiffening is necessary."
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

```turtle
gen:I2_060_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMember ;
        sh:class nltl:structuralMember ;
        sh:node gen:I2_060_StructuralMemberShape

        sh:path nltl:structuralStiffening ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A member in way of a cutout must not be unstable." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
    ?member nltl:hasCutout ?cutout .
    ?member nltl:memberInWayOfCutout true .
    FILTER (
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:memberInstability ?state .
            FILTER (?state = false)
        }
        || EXISTS {
            $this nltl:memberInstability true
        }
    )
}
"""
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Where stiffening is necessary for a member in way of a cutout, structural stiffening must be recorded." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
    ?member nltl:hasCutout ?cutout .
    ?member nltl:memberInWayOfCutout true .
    ?member nltl:stiffeningNecessary true .
    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        $this nltl:structuralStiffening ?stiffening .
        FILTER (datatype(?stiffening) = xsd:string)
    }
}
"""
    ] .

gen:I2_060_StructuralMemberShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:memberInWayOfCutout ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
```

Original source text:
The Manual shall include risk-based procedures for monitoring and maintaining safety during operations in ice, as applicable, including any requirements for escort operations or icebreaker assistance. Different operational limitations may apply depending on whether the ship is operating independently or with icebreaker escort. Where appropriate, the PWOM should specify both options.
Normalized requirement:
IF ice_operation_applicable = true, THEN PWOM shall include ice-operation monitoring and safety procedures, including applicable escort or icebreaker-assistance requirements and operating-mode-specific limitations.
Required inputs: PWOM, operation mode, escort requirements, and applicable limitations

Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?
Q7 Traceability / clarity: Is the implementation understandable and traceable to the cited evidence?

Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-098-20260915T112326114013Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
For ships intended to operate in low air temperature, all other survival craft shall: in order to be located, carry one device for transmitting signals for location; and for on-scene communications, carry one device for transmitting and receiving on-scene communications.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_operates_in_low_air_temperature = true, THEN every other survival craft shall carry at least one location-signal device and one on-scene transmit/receive communication device.
Required inputs: Operating-temperature class, craft inventory, and assigned device inventory

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO098OtherSurvivalCraftShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasLocationSignalDevice ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each other survival craft must carry at least one location-signal device."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasOnSceneCommunicationDevice ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each other survival craft must carry at least one on-scene transmit/receive communication device."
    ] .

gen:IMO098ApplicableShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInLowAirTemperature ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasOtherSurvivalCraft ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:node gen:IMO098OtherSurvivalCraftShape
    ] .

gen:IMO098NotApplicableShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInLowAirTemperature ;
        sh:hasValue false
    ] .

gen:IMO098ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInLowAirTemperature ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship's low-air-temperature operating status must be recorded as a single boolean value."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO098ApplicableShape
        gen:IMO098NotApplicableShape
    ) .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:IMO_106_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:dischargedMaterialClassification ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:dischargedMaterialTypeValue ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide one discharged material classification."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeOilDischargeParagraph1Point1Point1Applicable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For clean ballast or segregated ballast discharge, paragraph 1.1.1 must be recorded as not applicable." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:dischargedMaterialClassification ?material .
                FILTER (
                    ?material = nltl:cleanBallastMaterial ||
                    ?material = nltl:segregatedBallastMaterial
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:polarCodeOilDischargeParagraph1Point1Point1Applicable ?applicable .
                    FILTER (?applicable = false)
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO106ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:dischargedMaterialClassification ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A discharged material classification is required to determine whether the paragraph 1.1.1 prohibition applies."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeOilDischargeParagraph1Point1Point1Applicable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:message "The paragraph 1.1.1 applicability indicator must be a boolean."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For clean ballast or segregated ballast, paragraph 1.1.1 oil-discharge prohibition applicability must be false." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:dischargedMaterialClassification ?material .
                FILTER (
                    ?material = nltl:cleanBallastMaterial ||
                    ?material = nltl:segregatedBallastMaterial
                )
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:polarCodeOilDischargeParagraph1Point1Point1Applicable ?applicableFalse .
                        FILTER (?applicableFalse = false)
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:polarCodeOilDischargeParagraph1Point1Point1Applicable ?applicableOther .
                        FILTER (?applicableOther != false)
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

F. MANUAL REVIEW CHECKLIST — DO NOT PREFILL GRADES
Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?
Q7 Traceability / clarity: Is the implementation understandable and traceable to the cited evidence?

Original source text:
The capacity of the air compressors must be sufficient for charging the air receivers from atmospheric to full pressure in one (1) hour, except for a ship with ice class IA Super, if its propulsion engine has to be reversed for going astern, in which case the compressor must be able to charge the receivers in half an hour.
Normalized requirement:

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF128ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIaSuper
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
            nltl:iceClassIi
            nltl:iceClassIii
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must have one permitted ice class." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propulsionEngineReversalRequiredForAstern ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The propulsion-engine reversal condition for astern must be stated." ;
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:TRF128IaSuperReversalShape
        gen:TRF128IaSuperNoReversalShape
        gen:TRF128OtherIceClassShape
    ) .

gen:TRF128IaSuperReversalShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIaSuper ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propulsionEngineReversalRequiredForAstern ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:compressorChargeTime ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:maxInclusive "0.5"^^xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:HR ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "For ice class IA Super with propulsion-engine reversal for astern, compressor charge time must be at most 0.5 hour." ;
    ] .

gen:TRF128IaSuperNoReversalShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:iceClassIaSuper ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propulsionEngineReversalRequiredForAstern ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:compressorChargeTime ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:maxInclusive "1"^^xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:HR ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Except for the IA Super reversal case, compressor charge time must be at most 1 hour." ;
    ] .

gen:TRF128OtherIceClassShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
            nltl:iceClassIi
            nltl:iceClassIii
        ) ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:compressorChargeTime ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:maxInclusive "1"^^xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:HR ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "For other ice classes, compressor charge time must be at most 1 hour." ;
    ] .

```

Original source text:
For category A and B ships constructed on or after 1 January 2017 with an aggregate oil fuel capacity of less than 600 m^3, all oil fuel tanks shall be separated from the outer shell by a distance of not less than 0.76 m. This provision does not apply to small oil fuel tanks with a maximum individual capacity not greater than 30 m^3.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_category IN {'A','B'} AND construction_date >= '2017-01-01' AND aggregate_oil_fuel_capacity_m3 < 600 AND individual_oil_fuel_tank_capacity_m3 > 30, THEN oil_fuel_tank_outer_shell_separation_m >= 0.76.
Required inputs: Ship category/date and oil-fuel tank capacities/separation measurements

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_108_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship category is required to determine applicability of the oil-fuel-tank separation rule."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A valid construction date is required to determine applicability of the oil-fuel-tank separation rule."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:aggregateOilFuelCapacity ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M3 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Aggregate oil fuel capacity must be a single decimal quantity in cubic metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:individualOilFuelTankCapacity ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M3 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Individual oil fuel tank capacity must be a single decimal quantity in cubic metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:oilFuelTankOuterShellSeparation ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Oil fuel tank outer-shell separation must be a decimal quantity in metres when provided."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For a Category A or B ship constructed on or after 1 January 2017, with aggregate oil fuel capacity below 600 m3 and individual tank capacity above 30 m3, oil fuel tanks must be separated from the outer shell by at least 0.76 m." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category ;
                      nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate ;
                      nltl:aggregateOilFuelCapacity ?aggregateQuantity ;
                      nltl:individualOilFuelTankCapacity ?individualQuantity .

                ?aggregateQuantity qudt:numericValue ?aggregateCapacity ;
                                    qudt:unit unit:M3 .
                ?individualQuantity qudt:numericValue ?individualCapacity ;
                                    qudt:unit unit:M3 .

                FILTER (
                    ?category IN (
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                    )
                )
                FILTER (?constructionDate >= "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date)
                FILTER (?aggregateCapacity < 600)
                FILTER (?individualCapacity > 30)

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:oilFuelTankOuterShellSeparation ?separationQuantity .
                    ?separationQuantity qudt:numericValue ?separation ;
                                        qudt:unit unit:M .
                    FILTER (?separation >= 0.76)
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Original source text:
(ii) The waterline length of the bow region is generally to be divided into 4 sub-regions of equal length. The force (F), line load (Q), pressure (P) and load patch aspect ratio (AR) are to be calculated with respect to the mid-length position of each sub-region (each maximum of F, Q and P is to be used in the calculation of the ice load parameters Pavg, b and w).

```turtle
        sh:message "The four bow subregions must be equal-length, with calculation-case midpoints at one-eighth, three-eighths, five-eighths, and seven-eighths of bowLength" ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bowLength ?bowLength .
                ?bowLength qudt:numericValue ?bowLengthValue .
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?case .
                        ?case nltl:bowSubregionMidLengthPosition ?midpoint .
                        ?midpoint qudt:numericValue ?midpointValue .
                        FILTER (?midpointValue = (?bowLengthValue * 0.125))
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?case .
                        ?case nltl:bowSubregionMidLengthPosition ?midpoint .
                        ?midpoint qudt:numericValue ?midpointValue .
                        FILTER (?midpointValue = (?bowLengthValue * 0.375))
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?case .
                        ?case nltl:bowSubregionMidLengthPosition ?midpoint .
                        ?midpoint qudt:numericValue ?midpointValue .
                        FILTER (?midpointValue = (?bowLengthValue * 0.625))
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?case .
                        ?case nltl:bowSubregionMidLengthPosition ?midpoint .
                        ?midpoint qudt:numericValue ?midpointValue .
                        FILTER (?midpointValue = (?bowLengthValue * 0.875))
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:selectedMaximumBowForce ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:ForceQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:selectedMaximumBowLineLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:LineLoadQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:selectedMaximumBowPressure ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:PressureQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "selectedMaximumBowForce must be the maximum force reported for the bow subregions" ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:selectedMaximumBowForce ?selected .
                ?selected qudt:numericValue ?selectedValue .
                $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedForce ?force .
                ?force qudt:numericValue ?caseValue .
                FILTER (?caseValue > ?selectedValue)
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "selectedMaximumBowForce must equal a reported bow subregion maximum" ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:selectedMaximumBowForce ?selected .
                ?selected qudt:numericValue ?selectedValue .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?case .
                    ?case nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedForce ?force .
                    ?force qudt:numericValue ?caseValue .
                    FILTER (?caseValue = ?selectedValue)
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [

    ] ;

    ] ;

    ] ;
```

```turtle
    ] ;

    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The selected maximum bow force, line load, and pressure must equal the respective maxima reported for the four bow subregion calculation cases." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this
                    nltl:selectedMaximumBowForce ?selectedForceNode ;
                    nltl:selectedMaximumBowLineLoad ?selectedLineLoadNode ;
                    nltl:selectedMaximumBowPressure ?selectedPressureNode .

                ?selectedForceNode qudt:numericValue ?selectedForce .
                ?selectedLineLoadNode qudt:numericValue ?selectedLineLoad .
                ?selectedPressureNode qudt:numericValue ?selectedPressure .

                FILTER (
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?forceCase .
                        ?forceCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedForce ?forceNode .
                        ?forceNode qudt:numericValue ?forceValue .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?greaterForceCase .
                            ?greaterForceCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedForce ?greaterForceNode .
                            ?greaterForceNode qudt:numericValue ?greaterForce .
                            FILTER (?greaterForce > ?forceValue)
                        }
                        FILTER (?selectedForce != ?forceValue)
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?lineLoadCase .
                        ?lineLoadCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedLineLoad ?lineLoadNode .
                        ?lineLoadNode qudt:numericValue ?lineLoadValue .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?greaterLineLoadCase .
                            ?greaterLineLoadCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedLineLoad ?greaterLineLoadNode .
                            ?greaterLineLoadNode qudt:numericValue ?greaterLineLoad .
                            FILTER (?greaterLineLoad > ?lineLoadValue)
                        }
                        FILTER (?selectedLineLoad != ?lineLoadValue)
                    }
                    ||
```

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:selectedMaximumBowForce/qudt:numericValue ?selectedForce ;
                      nltl:selectedMaximumBowLineLoad/qudt:numericValue ?selectedLineLoad ;
                      nltl:selectedMaximumBowPressure/qudt:numericValue ?selectedPressure .

                FILTER (
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?caseForce .
                        ?caseForce nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedForce/qudt:numericValue ?force .
                        FILTER (?force > ?selectedForce)
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?caseLineLoad .
                        ?caseLineLoad nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedLineLoad/qudt:numericValue ?lineLoad .
                        FILTER (?lineLoad > ?selectedLineLoad)
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?casePressure .
                        ?casePressure nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedPressure/qudt:numericValue ?pressure .
                        FILTER (?pressure > ?selectedPressure)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?equalForceCase .
                        ?equalForceCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedForce/qudt:numericValue ?equalForce .
                        FILTER (?equalForce = ?selectedForce)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?equalLineLoadCase .
                        ?equalLineLoadCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedLineLoad/qudt:numericValue ?equalLineLoad .
                        FILTER (?equalLineLoad = ?selectedLineLoad)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasBowSubregionCalculationCase ?equalPressureCase .
                        ?equalPressureCase nltl:bowSubregionCalculatedPressure/qudt:numericValue ?equalPressure .
                        FILTER (?equalPressure = ?selectedPressure)
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Original source text:
(i) The average pressure, Pavg, within a design load patch is determined as follows: Pavg = F / (b * w) [MPa] where F = FBow or FNonBow as appropriate for the hull area under consideration [MN] b = bBow or bNonBow as appropriate for the hull area under consideration [m] w = wBow or wNonBow as appropriate for the hull area under consideration [m] (ii) Areas of higher, concentrated pressure exist within the load patch. In general, smaller areas have higher local pressures. Accordingly, the peak pressure factors listed in Table 3 are used to account for the pressure concentration on localized structural members.

            ]

```turtle
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:peakPressureFactor ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [

                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;

                sh:maxCount 1

                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]

    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Average ice pressure must equal ice force divided by load patch height and length, and the reported peak pressure factor must match the selected Table 3 factor within numerical tolerance." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceForce ?force ;
                      nltl:loadPatchHeight ?height ;
                      nltl:loadPatchLength ?length ;
                      nltl:averageIcePressure ?average ;
                      nltl:peakPressureFactor ?peak ;
                      nltl:hasPeakPressureFactorLookupCase ?lookupCase .

                ?force qudt:numericValue ?forceValue .
                ?height qudt:numericValue ?heightValue .
                ?length qudt:numericValue ?lengthValue .
                ?average qudt:numericValue ?averageValue .
                ?peak qudt:numericValue ?peakValue .
                ?lookupCase nltl:selectedPeakPressureFactor ?selected .
                ?selected qudt:numericValue ?selectedValue .

                FILTER (?heightValue != 0)
                FILTER (?lengthValue != 0)

                BIND ((?forceValue / (?heightValue * ?lengthValue)) / 1000 AS ?expectedAverage)

                FILTER (
                    (ABS(?averageValue - ?expectedAverage) > 0.000001 &&
                     ABS(?averageValue - ?expectedAverage) > (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedAverage)))
                    ||
                    (ABS(?peakValue - ?selectedValue) > 0.000001 &&
                     ABS(?peakValue - ?selectedValue) > (0.000001 * ABS(?selectedValue)))
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
gs:I2_018_ShipShape

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:peakPressureFactor ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Average ice pressure must equal ice force divided by load patch height and length, converted from kN/m2 to MPa, within the permitted numerical tolerance." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceForce ?forceQuantity ;
                      nltl:loadPatchHeight ?heightQuantity ;
                      nltl:loadPatchLength ?lengthQuantity ;
                      nltl:averageIcePressure ?pressureQuantity .
                ?forceQuantity qudt:numericValue ?force .
                ?heightQuantity qudt:numericValue ?height .
                ?lengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?length .
                ?pressureQuantity qudt:numericValue ?reported .
                BIND ((?force * 0.001) / (?height * ?length) AS ?expected)
                BIND (ABS(?reported - ?expected) AS ?difference)
                FILTER (
                    ?difference > (ABS(?expected) * 0.000001)
                    &&
                    ?difference > 0.000000000001
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-070-20260915T133019503006Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The regulations also apply to azimuthing and fixed thrusters for main propulsion, taking consideration of loads resulting from propeller/ice interaction and loads on the thruster body/ice interaction. The given azimuthing thruster body loads are the expected ice loads for the ship's service life under normal operational conditions. The local strength of the thruster body shall be sufficient to withstand local ice pressure when the thruster body is designed for extreme loads.
Normalized requirement:
The regulations also apply to azimuthing and fixed thrusters for main propulsion, taking consideration of loads resulting from propeller/ice interaction and loads on the thruster body/ice interaction. The given azimuthing thruster body loads are the expected ice loads for the ship's service life under normal operational conditions. The local strength of the thruster body shall be sufficient to withstand local ice pressure when the thruster body is designed for extreme loads.
Required inputs: No explicit data variable identified

```turtle
gen:ForceKiloNewtonShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;

        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gen:PressureMegaPascalShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gen:TRF070Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:mainPropulsionThruster ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:thrusterType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:mainPropulsionThruster ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:thrusterType ;
                sh:in ( "azimuthing" "fixed" )
            ] ;

                sh:node gen:ForceKiloNewtonShape ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;

                sh:path nltl:thrusterBodyIceInteractionLoad ;
                sh:node gen:ForceKiloNewtonShape ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:localIcePressure ;
                sh:node gen:PressureMegaPascalShape ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [

                sh:minCount 1 ;

                        sh:node gen:ForceKiloNewtonShape ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:sparql [
                        sh:message "The design condition local-strength capacity must be compared with the applicable local ice pressure only after both operands have been represented in compatible units." ;
                        sh:select "PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> SELECT $this WHERE { $this nltl:designConditionLocalStrengthCapacity ?capacity . ?capacity qudt:numericValue ?capacityValue ; qudt:unit ?capacityUnit . ?ship nltl:hasDesignCondition $this ; nltl:localIcePressure ?pressure . ?pressure qudt:numericValue ?pressureValue ; qudt:unit ?pressureUnit .                     ]
```

```turtle
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-070> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:mainPropulsionThruster ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:mainPropulsionThruster ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:mainPropulsionThruster ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:thrusterType ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:in ( "azimuthing" "fixed" ) ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerIceInteractionLoad ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:thrusterBodyIceInteractionLoad ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:localIcePressure ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaPA> ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasDesignCondition ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class nltl:designCondition ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:designConditionExpectedLoad ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:designConditionLocalStrengthCapacity ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Each design condition local-strength capacity must be at least the ship's local ice pressure." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:hasDesignCondition ?condition ;
                              nltl:localIcePressure ?pressure .
                        ?condition nltl:designConditionLocalStrengthCapacity ?capacity .
                        ?pressure qudt:numericValue ?pressureValue .
                        ?capacity qudt:numericValue ?capacityValue .
                        FILTER ( ?capacityValue < ?pressureValue )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

```turtle
g:TRF070Shape a sh:NodeShape ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:mainPropulsionThruster ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;

                        sh:path nltl:designConditionLocalStrengthCapacity ;
                        sh:node [
```
